Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the probabilities structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 438. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 337 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

This completes the proof. QED.
